## Patterns Learned

### Source-based heuristics
- **AppSumo / LTD-heavy source** → almost always sub-$500K ARR. Default fit 1–2 unless contact data confirms ICP. The LTD business model signals a price-sensitive SMB customer base that does not fit $30K engagement.
- **Y Combinator + agent-ready stance** (e.g. Activepieces, PLG OS) → they are the disruptor, not the disrupted. Score 1–2 — no gap to fill.
- **ProductHunt launches at pre-seed / seed** with funding <$2M → usually sub-ICP ARR. Note for 12-month revisit.
- **Bootstrapped founders with public revenue milestones on LinkedIn** (Marko Saric, Jane Portman, Geoff Roberts, Mike Kulakov) → high fit signal. Reachability and personalization hooks are strong.

### AI posture scoring
- **`none` posture in a category peers are actively AI-bolting** (Userlist, Savio, Outseta, Dubsado, Everhour vs Timely) → highest fit. These are the "we need to figure out what AI to build" prospects.
- **`bolt-on` posture on real workflow-heavy products** (Docupilot, Kickserv, Noloco, Simple Analytics, TenantCloud/Cloudia) → fit 3–4. Room to re-architect AI around workflow vs feature bolt-on.
- **`strategic` posture with agent-readiness gap** → fit 3. We can add value on the agent surface even if AI-features are sorted.
- **`agent-ready` posture** (Papermark, Tally w/ MCP, Activepieces, PLG OS, Stacker post-rebrand) → fit 1–2. Ahead of curve, not the target.

### Founder reachability signals
- Founders who podcast / blog / post LinkedIn regularly (UI Breakfast, Makerpad, Indie Hackers features) score +1 on fit because hooks are abundant and response-rate is higher.
- Founders with prior exit or recognizable prior company (Derrick Reimer ex-Drip, Laura Roeder ex-MeetEdgar, Mark DeHaan ex-Rentler) score +1 on hook strength.

### Category-level warnings
- **Customer support AI** (helpdesk, chatbots) is crowded with well-funded AI-native competitors (Fin, Decagon). Score harshly — incumbents like ThriveDesk/Charla don't move.
- **Privacy-first analytics** is a strong category target (small team, workflow-exposed, AI-posture usually exploring or bolt-on). BUT: bootstrapped 2-person privacy analytics companies (Fathom, Plausible) can quietly grow past the $5M ICP ceiling with tiny teams. Employee count is a misleading ICP proxy in this category — always check revenue first.
- **Email/lifecycle marketing for SaaS** is an AI-gap category — peers like Customer.io/HubSpot push AI hard, leaving Userlist-style bootstrapped SaaS exposed.
- **Construction/FSM** — workflow-heavy but the industry adopts slowly; founders reachable but conversion may be long.
- **Property management SaaS** (TenantCloud, Buildium, AppFolio) — workflow-heavy vertical with multi-million ARR mid-market players that are bolting on thin GPT wrappers (Cloudia-style listing generators). Treat similar to construction/FSM: high-fit for assessment but longer sales cycles. AI bolt-on leaves big workflow-redesign space.
- **Time tracking embedded in PM tools** — Everhour-style bootstrapped tools are exposed because (a) Timely is AI-native, (b) the PM platforms they embed into are adding their own AI. Both FRS angles (AI-for-users + agent-readiness) apply naturally.
- **Product Management SaaS** (Freshflows, Productboard, Canny, Aha!) — AI-native roadmap copilots dominate category discussion; sub-ICP bootstrapped entrants can't catch up without capital. Score low unless ARR clearly inside range.
- **Constraint-satisfaction verticals are the single most reliable fit-4 shape — 3/3 (assignr, sportskey, jersey-watch).** Any product whose hard job is allocating scarce resources across competing constraints (referees, facility slots, league fields x divisions x blackout dates x officials). **Tell: the vendor markets the hard part as a SPEED claim — "build complex schedules in minutes" — which means it is solved by a fast generator, not by reasoning.** Gap + named reachable founder = 4; gap + anonymous vendor = 3.
- **Compliance-heavy regulated verticals are the durable `none`-posture goldmine** (qt9 eQMS, infotouch POS, cemsites deathcare, buildingreports-style fire/life-safety). Generic horizontal AI never reaches FDA/ISO/HIPAA/excise workflows, so the incumbent's missing AI is durable rather than negligent — and peers still market AI hard at the same buyers.
- **AI-native rival running a NAMED CONQUEST/COMPARISON PAGE against the prospect is the strongest competitive hook shape found so far** (flex-rental-solutions vs Stagera at `stagera.ai/compare/flex/`). This is one level sharper than the bidwrangler "AI-native point-solution integrates into your platform" signal: an integration implies encroachment, a conquest page is an explicit, dated, quotable attack that names the prospect and lists the exact weaknesses (on-premise/hybrid deployment, per-module add-on pricing) the AI-native rival is selling against. **Search `<competitor>.ai/compare/<prospect>` and "<prospect> vs" for every none-posture prospect — it costs one search and converts abstract disruption talk into evidence the founder can verify in one click.**
- **Competitor comparison pages are also a negative-confirmation source for AI posture** (buildingreports). Inspect Point and ZenTrades comparison content asserting they alone have "AI-powered inspections"/"AI scoring" and framing the prospect as documentation-only is third-party evidence of the prospect's AI gap — valuable when the vendor's own site is unreachable. Same family as the SourceForge "no AI capabilities" field used on stonegrid.
- **A large regulated inspection/audit history is a genuine data moat, not just scale bragging** (buildingreports: 120M+ devices, 1.85M inspections). Device-level pass/fail history over decades is exactly the substrate for predictive failure and deficiency triage, and it maps straight onto deliverable #3. **When a compliance vendor quotes a huge cumulative inspection/transaction count, score it as a moat asset and lead outreach with it.**

### Disqualifying signals
- **Full AI-native rebrand** (domain change to .ai, prompt-to-app UX as landing page, "AI-native platform" positioning) is the clearest disqualifying signal. Stacker's 2025 rebrand to stacker.ai with prompt-to-app launch is textbook agent-ready = fit 1.
- **a16z Series A + AI pivot** → almost always above ICP and pushing for implementation not assessment. Fit 1.
- **Founder retirement / transition in progress** (Paul Jarvis from Fathom Apr 2026) → buyer is distracted, decision-making fragmented, timing poor. Downgrade fit by 1.
- **AI already shipped workflow-first on a micro-budget** (crosstrax) → fit 2. Do not reward a long AI feature list; ask whether it CLOSES the gap. Pair with the ratio test below.
- **Engagement-as-%-of-revenue test — run on every sub-$1M prospect.** $30K against ~$800K revenue is ~4% of annual revenue: impossible regardless of fit. Clearing the $500K floor is necessary, not sufficient.
- **SaaS + custom-development services hybrid** (auctionmethod) → sub-floor tell. Signals product ARR too small to live on AND a roadmap fragmented across client work. Same category can legitimately split 4 vs 2 on scale alone — state the in-batch contrast in the not-a-fit's fit_assessment.
- **Aggregator ARR conflicts resolve via price-point arithmetic, and a ~6x spread is normal** (iris-works: $5.9M vs $500K-$1M from different aggregators). At $9.95-$25/mo, $5.9M needs ~20,000 subscribers — implausible for a niche CRM behind a funded leader, and irreconcilable with 4-8 employees. **Take the low figure, then run the engagement-as-%-of-revenue test: it killed this one at ~4% of revenue.** Reconfirms crosstrax — clearing the $500K floor is necessary, not sufficient.
- **Partner-delivered AI still counts as the prospect's AI posture** (bestnotes: Videra Sidekick Notes + Perspectives AI, both partnerships). It is tempting to discount integrated third-party AI as "not really theirs" and score the gap as open. Do not. The buyer's answer to "what should we build?" is already settled, and they publish the ROI metric (47,500 clinician hours saved yr 1). **Comprehensive AI on the core transactional surface = no discovery gap, regardless of who built it.** Same kill as cybake/CatsAI.

### Refinements from 2026-08-11

- **Constraint-satisfaction verticals are now 4/4 (assignr, sportskey, jersey-watch, usked).** Usked extends the shape beyond sports/officials into *credentialed professional services*: allocating scarce sign-language interpreters across language, certification level, subject expertise, location, availability and compliance. **The vertical does not matter; the shape does — any product whose hard job is matching scarce credentialed humans to constrained demand slots.** Every instance has scored 4. Add "credentialing/certification compliance" to the tells alongside the SPEED-claim marketing tell.
- **AI-native CONQUEST PAGE reconfirmed as the strongest disruption evidence — 2/2 (flex-rental-solutions/Stagera, smart145/AIquote).** It produced this system's first fit-5 in six runs. AIquote's page names Smart145 and states the exact weakness it sells against ("quoting is not Smart145's strength… operational, not commercial"). **Run the `<competitor>.ai` + "<prospect> vs" search on EVERY none-posture prospect — one query, and when it hits it is worth roughly a full point of fit score** because it converts "AI might disrupt you" into a dated artifact the founder can verify in one click. Also check whether the conquest page vendor targets *other* products in the category (AIquote also targets Quantum-MX) — a category-wide land-grab is a stronger urgency argument than a single attack.
- **A conquest page attacking ONE SURFACE is a fit signal; a rival replacing the CORE is a disqualifier.** AIquote attacks only quoting, leaving Smart145's MRO franchise intact — attacked but not boxed in, which is exactly the open window the rubric's fit-5 wants. Distinguish "encroachment on a module" from "AI-native replacement of the product" before scoring; they point in opposite directions.
- **SPLIT THE FOUNDER-TRANSITION DISQUALIFIER: completed handover ≠ announced transition.** The existing rule (downgrade 1 for founder retirement/transition, per Fathom/Paul Jarvis) assumed a distracted buyer. That holds for **announced/in-progress** transitions (agtrax — "Announces Leadership Transition", downgraded). It does NOT hold for a **completed** handover to a long-tenured successor (textile-technologies — Jan 2026, founder Joe Dulworth → Dawn Vaughn, 25 years with the customers and codebase). A new owner in year one owns the roadmap, is motivated to modernise, and the handover itself is a strong dated hook. **Ask "is the transition finished, and is the successor an insider?" — if yes, treat as neutral-to-positive, not a downgrade.**
- **Hardware + catalog hybrid is a sub-floor tell, same family as the SaaS+custom-dev hybrid** (forestry-systems: handheld log scalers/tally devices sold via catalog alongside software). Revenue is device sales and perpetual licences, so an ARR figure in the ICP sense does not exist and the engagement-as-%-of-revenue test fails on a small team funding a manufacturing line. **Tell: a "Hardware" or "Catalog" nav item on the site, or trade-press coverage of a device product line.** The underlying workflow can still be great AI territory (computer-vision log scaling) — score the business model, not the problem.
- **Price-point arithmetic beat the sheet again (cyanic-automation) — and this time the sheet was simply wrong.** Published $10–$40/user/mo means the sheet's `$810K` needs ~1,700–6,750 seats for a 6-person Edmonton vendor in a narrow niche. **When the arithmetic contradicts `arr_estimate`, trust the arithmetic and flag the sheet row for correction** — do not silently score around it. Reachable, content-producing founders (Kondor: blog, conference talks, podcast) do NOT rescue a sub-floor ARR; mark for 12-month revisit instead.
- **Discard growjo-style revenue estimates that contradict headcount** (frontrunner-professional: "$500K" against 40–50 employees, 30 years trading, "one of the largest funeral technology companies"). This is the aggregator-conflict rule (iris-works, ~6x spread) extended: when a figure is not merely low but *internally impossible* against headcount and tenure, discard it entirely rather than averaging it in — then flag ARR as the open question.
- **Acquirer/consolidator posture is a soft negative.** frontrunner-professional bought TerraPro's source code and customer base; grossman-software sells customised enterprise solutions. Companies buying competitors or running consulting-led delivery are more consolidator than disrupted incumbent, and their AI question is a build-vs-buy portfolio decision, not a discovery gap. Cap at 3.
- **Cross-product AI assistant that reaches OUT into the customer's own systems = strategic/agent-ready, disqualify** (avontus: one assistant spanning Quantify/Designer/Viewer/ScaffoldIQ *plus* customer CRM, accounting, SharePoint, knowledge bases). This is a level beyond a per-product bolt-on and is exactly the engagement's own output, already shipped and announced. **Tell: the AI announcement lists external systems, not just internal modules.** Keep these as category proof points for outreach to their vertical peers.

## Open Questions / Flags
- Many prospects (ninjapipe, heffl, flowlu, sociamonials, guideflow, plgos, charla, activepieces, thrivedesk, docupilot, dubsado, paperbell, kickserv, jobtread) came in without contact data. Sourcer must backfill contact_name / contact_linkedin before outreach writer can use the scored rows. Even fit 4–5 prospects are unreachable without those fields.
- freshflows and stacker from 2026-04-22 run also came in without contact data. Moot since both scored fit 1, but note the pattern: G2Capterra and JobBoards sources tend to arrive without contacts.

- **BIMODAL-VERTICAL PATTERN EXTENDED — a vertical can miss the band on BOTH sides at once (backflow/cross-connection compliance).** `backflow-solutions` (BSI Online) = **2, ABOVE ceiling** (Clodura $11M-$100M, ~51 staff, 30+ yrs, 26 states, patented tracking system) **and** a services hybrid (contracted to RUN municipal programs = service revenue, zhenhub/rx4route pattern) — reachable 40-year founder Michael Eisenhauer who co-authored Illinois backflow regs does not rescue it. `ecosconnect` = **2, likely BELOW floor** (handful of small PNW municipalities, zero revenue/employee data anywhere, no named owner, owner-operator side-product of a backflow testing business). **Neither in band.** New note: when a batch delivers two players in one niche vertical, check both against the band before assuming one is the incumbent-vs-AI-native pair.
- **BAKERY = 4th consecutive incumbent-vs-AI-forward pair (bakesmart 3 / cybake 2).** `cybake` (RedBlack Software, UK, Jane Tyler MD since 1998) = **2 on two independent caps: $5.1M rev 2025 (at/over ceiling) AND a shipped strategic AI bet on the core surface** — the **CatsAI partnership embeds predictive AI into the live product**, marketed as a world-first, plus "Cybake Instore — power of prediction". **Demand forecasting/waste IS the bakery margin problem, so this is tekmon/inflatableoffice: comprehensive AI on the core transactional surface = no discovery gap.** `bakesmart` = **3** (Indianapolis, since 2005, founder Mike Clements): ships only **"AI Checklists" (auto operational + food-safety checklists) = narrow and ADJACENT, so the Pinty rule applies and it does NOT cap**, while production planning, demand forecasting, waste and the static "Cake Matrix" custom-cake pricing stay manual. **Cybake makes BakeSmart's competitive-moat pitch unusually sharp — cite the rival by name.**
- **AI-CHECKLISTS = a new confirmed instance of the Pinty "adjacent general AI does not cap" rule.** Auto-generating operational/food-safety checklists sits beside the workflow, not on the transactional surface. Contrast within the same vertical: CatsAI demand prediction (core surface) = cap to 2.
- **ezshred reconfirms the scrapright/trash-flow waste-mgmt-ops family at the datascan-3 floor.** Document destruction/shredding (Chesterland OH, built 2000 to run a shredder's own ops, 300+ clients, i-SIGMA exhibitor). Regulated **chain-of-custody + certificates of destruction + barcoded asset tracking + volatile scrap-paper commodity pricing** = the exact scrapright margin shape, completely un-AI'd, no AI-native entrant in document destruction. **Google Maps route optimisation = algorithmic, lawnpro rule, does NOT count as AI.** Held at 3 (borderline 2) by three caps: no named owner anywhere + conflicting revenue ($4M vs 3 employees from the same directory family) + legacy desktop lineage ("Route Tracker V4", 2000 origin) = trash-flow/wam-software pattern.
- **NEW/REINFORCED cap — the SHEET'S OWN arr_estimate can be internally impossible; sanity-check it against price point (escape-room-master fit 2).** Sheet said **3M-5M with employee_count = 2** at a **$69/month** price point — $3M+ would need several thousand escape-room venues, more than the global market supports (and The Org says 11-50 employees, contradicting the sheet again). **Divide claimed ARR by the published price to get implied customer count and compare it to the vertical's total addressable venue count — fastest ARR lie-detector available.** Also applied the multi-product founder cap: owner **Nate Shane runs 5+ businesses** (Wicked Escapes, Cracked STL, Paintball Sallys, NaTech Airsoft, consulting). Ironically the **only prospect in the batch with a LinkedIn already in the sheet**. Revisit 12mo.
- **golf-oclock reconfirms drive-scout/pembee thin-booking = fit 2, and that "boom category" is a NEGATIVE at seed stage.** Montreal indoor-golf/simulator booking + bay access + memberships + POS, 200+ venues, named reachable co-founder-CEO **Alexandre Martin** (+ JC Delage), clean none posture, active PR (**Indoor Golf Alliance technology sponsor, Nov-2025**). But public footprint only dates to 2024, 200 venues at SMB pricing = likely sub-floor, and the **tee-sheet optimiser / auto simulator allocation are rules-based helpers, not workflow depth**. **Indoor golf being a boom category (19.1M Americans) actively HURTS — it pulls funded entrants (Teelogix launched 2025, 8+ platforms) in faster than a seed-stage incumbent can differentiate.** Contrast streamways (4, deep driving-school ops) vs this. Revisit 12mo.
- Contact gaps: **all 5 fit-3+ rows have blank contact_email AND blank contact_linkedin.** Worse than usual — **3 of 5 have NO named human at all**: `assignr` (no owner findable anywhere despite 16 yrs), `ezshred` (no owner findable), `bakesmart` (contact_name BLANK in sheet; founder **Mike Clements** found only in research). Named but LI missing from sheet: `curbside-laundries` (Aaron Simmons; personal LI found in research = linkedin.com/in/aaron-simmons-99a19a58/ — backfill), `stonegrid` (Raj Katta, technical co-founder — verify economic buyer). **Outreach Writer should SKIP assignr/ezshred/bakesmart rather than fabricate a recipient.**
- **SCHEMA INCONSISTENCY (re-reported, 5th consecutive run):** agent-def step 8 lists ai_posture + fit_score in the research_cache upsert, but live schema + actual tab = research_cache **A-L only** (prospect_id → recommended_angle). Reference wins → both written to prospects tab only (cols L, M). **This has now been reported five runs running and never fixed — escalate to the operator to amend the agent definition rather than re-reporting.**
- Env: fresh container; `pip install --user cffi cryptography` still required before sheet.py works (only cffi/pycparser missing; cryptography present). Creds pre-provisioned; FRS_GOOGLE_CREDENTIALS + Sheet ID pre-set. staleness=90. All 20 writes (10 cache + 10 prospects) OK via python subprocess driver (arg-list, avoids bash $-expansion on ARR $-values), 3s pacing, single researcher, no 429s. **fit_notes ran over the 200-char cap on 4 rows and needed a second corrective update pass — measure len() BEFORE writing, not after.**
### 2026-07-28 — all-identified (10 prospects, created_at-asc slice: all 4 of 07-14 + first 6 of 07-21)
- See `agents/research-runs/2026-07-28-10-prospects.md` — 6 researched(fit3+) / 4 not-a-fit / 0 cache hits / avg 2.90 / dist 1:0 2:4 3:3 4:3 5:0. No fit-5. **23 real `identified` at start** (read returned 62; 39 DELETED-* tombstones filtered client-side). Cleared all of 07-14 + 6 of 07-21. **NEXT slice** = `stonegrid` (last of 07-21, stone-fabrication — pair with actionflow findings) then the full 07-28 cluster(12). Spread: 07-14(4->0), 07-21(7->1), 07-28(12). 13 real identified remain.
- **NEW DISTINCTION — search-fund acquisition is NOT the PE-roll-up disqualifier (routegenie fit 4).** Cameron Craig raised a **Northspring Partners search fund to acquire RouteGenie in 2025** and is now owner-CEO. Unlike Access Group/Banyan/SureSwift/Constellation/Inktavo (product absorbed into a portfolio, roadmap controlled by parent), a search fund is a **single-asset owner-operator with fresh capital and a growth mandate** — historically a high-intent strategy buyer in year 1-2. Do NOT apply the acquired-company cap. Counter-risk to note: investor payback pressure can push toward implementation over assessment, so cap at 4 not 5.
- **NEW fit-4 AI-gap vertical: NEMT / non-emergency medical transportation (routegenie).** Buffalo NY, founded 2017, ~41 staff, 600+ fleets / 20,000+ vehicles / 46 states / ~2.5M trips-per-month dataset, in-band 1M-3M. HIGH regulated workflow (broker trip intake, eligibility, multi-load dispatch, GPS, **Medicaid broker claims reconciliation = the category margin killer, entirely un-AI'd**). `bolt-on`: markets "AI-powered" + published its own blog post *"Artificial Intelligence: An Exciting New Frontier for the NEMT Industry"* **while shipping nothing** = textbook what-to-build. DispatchGenie auto multi-loading = algorithmic (lawnpro route rule, does NOT cap). CEO authors the blog + "Inside NEMT" podcast guest = reachable.
- **INCUMBENT-vs-AI-NATIVE PAIR reconfirmed a third time (cattlemax/cattleytics → routegenie/tobi-cloud).** Same batch delivered both sides of NEMT: **routegenie = 4** (bolt-on incumbent, pitch competitive-moat) and **tobi-cloud = 2** (AI-native challenger: ML engine that learns trip patterns + AI dispatch optimization = core positioning, NOT bolt-on). **Pattern is now reliable enough to actively look for: when the sourcer delivers two companies in one vertical, check which is the incumbent and which is AI-native before scoring either.** tobi-cloud also stacked wrong-stage (2018, <10 staff, single $2M Allison Wood Ventures seed, zero revenue signal).
- **NEW cap — CLAUDE CONNECTOR / MCP connector = hardest agent-ready disqualifier yet (firm360 fit 2).** Accounting practice-mgmt (founded 2019 by working CPA Brandon Gray) shipped a **Claude Connector** letting partners query live firm data in plain language, claimed first in category. This is **waveortho/WaveyBeta squared**: conversational AI-for-users deliverable AND external agent surface, both shipped, in one feature. Excellent workflow depth + founder story do not rescue. **Generalize: any shipped MCP/LLM-vendor connector = agent-ready = fit 2 automatically, regardless of ARR or reachability.** Category (Karbon/TaxDome) is among the most AI-aggressive in vertical SaaS.
- **boatyard/waveortho shipped-AI cap reconfirmed on a perfect-ARR prospect (spacebring fit 2).** Coworking/flex-office (ex-andcards), **$2.4M rev / 22 staff = dead-centre ICP**, 500+ spaces / 50+ countries, named reachable co-founders (Igor Dzhebyan, Ross Khanas). Launched branded **"Lem AI agent"** Mar-2026 (support-ticket triage + data retrieval + content drafting) = our AI-enhanced-experience deliverable already built and marketed. **Perfect ARR does not rescue a shipped branded agent.** Best 12-month revisit in the batch if Lem stalls at ticket triage.
- **NEW fit-4 AI-gap vertical: countertop / stone fabrication shop mgmt (actionflow).** In-band 1M-3M, 100+ clients, ~$200-350/mo, mid-to-large + multi-location shops. HIGH workflow (quote → template → slab nesting → CNC/DXF → fabrication → install → multi-site). Clean **`none`** posture and **no AI-native player in the entire vertical** (Moraware incumbent, SlabWise the newer content-aggressive entrant). Founder **Reese Watt** = programmer + 7 yrs in the stone industry, wrote "JOT" for Hallmark Stone 2004 then rebuilt ActionFlow 2012 (Slippery Rock Gazette profile).
- **NEW HIGH-VALUE PATTERN — "rule-based automation identity" is the WARMEST none-posture shape (actionflow).** Their whole brand is automation, but it is a **trigger-and-action workflow builder every rule of which a human must author**. The buyer **already believes automation creates value**, so the pitch is not "why automate" but "your rules are static and someone who already knows the answer has to write each one" = shortest distance from `none` to an agentic blueprint. **Score this shape ABOVE a plain none-posture prospect, not below it — do not mistake a rules engine for a bolt-on AI feature.**
- **fflboss cap RESOLVED upward by a sibling — firearms/FFL-ATF compliance is a real fit-4 vertical when the caps clear (fastbound fit 4).** Confirmed **~$3.9M revenue** (Kona Equity, Feb-2024 — in-band but 2 yrs stale, verify not past ceiling), **bootstrapped 16 yrs with zero funding**, ~20 staff, 1B+ transactions, named co-founder **Jarad Haselton (former FFL dealer + competitive shooter = authentic founder-market fit)**. Clean `none` posture, no AI-native rival in the category. **Clears every cap fflboss (2026-07-24, held 3) was held down on: named owner, confirmed in-band ARR, real headcount, long track record.** Killer opening: **compliance software records correctly but never predicts ATF-inspection exposure** — anomaly detection across A&D entries + inspection-readiness scoring. Note their API-in-the-middle position (embedded in Celerant/AIM POS) gives partial agent-surface maturity = a reason to cap at 4.
- **NEW vertical + reconfirm of the owner-operator-side-product cap: self-service auto-recycling yard mgmt (yardsmart fit 3).** Cloud yard mgmt for u-pull-it yards; data-heavy (VIN, part interchange, yard location, sales velocity, scrap pricing) in a category run on **legacy desktop software with zero AI** (Car-Part.com Checkmate/Powerlink interchange monopoly, Hollander/Solera). **Elite reachability**: Troy Webber, third-generation owner of Chesterfield Auto Parts, public LI `linkedin.com/in/troy-webber/`, TWO Authority Magazine interviews, RVATech "Innovation in Cloud Computing" 2022, recurring Auto Recycling World coverage. **Held at 3 by: ARR/headcount both unknown with real sub-floor risk (a few hundred US self-service yards TAM) + the software is his SECOND business behind three operating yards** (AgriCommerce attention-split family). Lifts to 4 on confirmed in-band ARR.
- **zhenhub services+software hybrid cap applied twice this run.** `rx4route` **fit 3** (pharmacy last-mile SaaS paired with **Rx2Go**, a 1,000-driver delivery SERVICE — headline revenue is not clean SaaS ARR; founder split across both). `roverpass` **fit 3** (campground PMS paired with a **consumer campground booking MARKETPLACE** — same shape, marketplace commissions are not SaaS ARR; stacked with a **hired non-founder CEO** and ~$12.5M raised).
- **NEW EVIDENCE-QUALITY RULE — syndicated-PR-only profiles are not verification (rx4route).** Every scale claim (70,000 deliveries/day, 585+ pharmacies, 15M cumulative, "multi-million-dollar") traced ONLY to paid-PR syndication outlets (**ceoweekly, usinsider, usreporter, lawire, inkl, digitaljournal**). When a founder's entire footprint is this outlet family, treat ARR as **unverified**, cap at 3, and **tell the outreach writer not to quote the figures back** — being corrected on a self-reported number kills the message.
- **roastertools = clean sub-floor 2 (getlatka $316.3K / 3-person team / ~10 yrs / $237K raised; Owler confirms <$1M).** Coffee-roasting production + green-inventory + blend costing is a fine un-AI'd data-heavy domain (Cropster is the capitalized rival) — the domain is right, the stage is not. **Ten years to $316K reads as market ceiling, not slow execution** — flag 12-month revisit only, low expectation.
- **AI-spent-on-the-wrong-thing is a POSITIVE fit signal, not a posture disqualifier (roverpass).** Their public 2026 roadmap is **"AI-accelerated development"** (AI to ship custom features faster), plus an **"AI-enhanced marketing campaign"**, plus a **Zapier hand-off** exposing ChatGPT/Gemini/Claude to park owners — i.e. **AI in the dev process and the marketing, none in the operator's workflow**, and the one shipped AI surface outsources the thinking to the customer. Best opener of the batch: *"what is AI doing for the park owner at 7am on a Saturday?"* Score this as `bolt-on`, NOT strategic.

Sourcer must backfill before Outreach Writer.

Reference wins → both fields written to prospects tab only (cols L, M).

Multiple bootstrapped vertical/marketplace SaaS in $1-3M range with named reachable founders and zero AI in AI-bolting categories.
- **K1 Investment Management = disqualifier (growth-PE)** — RentRedi Series A $12M led by K1 ($17M+ raised, 395% 2-yr growth). Add K1 to PE-acquirer disqualifier list (alongside SureSwift/Constellation/Cordance/Tiny/Awesome Motive/Saas.group/Pantastic/Inverness Graham).
- **MCP/agent-ready disqualifier hit 5x this run**: WPManageNinja (FluentCRM v3 MCP for talk-to-leads), Etlworks (AI agent + REST API tools-of-agents), Storeganise (AI Connector to ChatGPT/Claude), BotPenguin (multi-LLM AI Agent Platform), QuoteIQ (AI Autopilot voice-command across 35 tools). The agent-ready bucket is growing fast in 2026.
- **AI-as-positioning vs AI-as-shipped = exploring vs bolt-on** — Juntrax markets "AI-powered office management" but no shipped features; Bonzai CEO "future AI-driven developments." Both scored 4 (exploring) because the FRS conversation is wide open. Rule: when AI is on the marketing page but not in the product, that's `exploring`, not `bolt-on`. Reserve `bolt-on` for shipped-but-tacked-on AI features.
- **9 staff at $3-5M ARR = extreme operational leverage = fit-5 signal** — Beds24 (Mark Kinchin) is textbook. Small team + bootstrapped + 20yrs ops + at-ceiling ARR + AI-empty in AI-bolting category = cleanest fit-5 since Aero Workflow/Financial Cents/Sweft. Default any prospect with "tiny team + significant revenue + decades-old + AI-empty" to fit 5.
- **YC W16 (10yrs ago) does NOT disqualify** — Yardbook YC W16 but functionally bootstrapped now, founder Mark Ke ex-Yahoo Engineering Manager still operating. Rule: YC batches 5+ years old should be treated as bootstrapped unless there's evidence of active fundraising. Distinct from current/recent YC batches (Activepieces, PLG OS) which remain agent-ready disruptors.
- **Latka vs founder-interview ARR discrepancy** — Clockodo Latka $1.2M but founder Happy Bootstrapping April 2024 says €500K MRR (~$6.5M ARR). When sources disagree by 5x, trust the more recent founder-interview number. Default to fit 3 with ARR-ambiguity caveat.
- **Practical Founders Podcast = strong reachability signal** — BIS Safety (Dan MacDonald) and Tryinteract (Josh Haynam) both surfaced from PFP. Cohort consistently has reachable bootstrapped founders. Caveat: PFP can also surface companies that crossed $5M ceiling (Tryinteract $5.3M = just above).
- **Branded AI-flagship feature = strategic cap at fit 3** — Interact AI Quiz Maker, QuoteIQ AI Autopilot, Storeganise AI Booking Assistant. When the product has a branded AI feature page, treat as strategic and cap at fit 3 (or lower if agent-ready or above-ICP).
- **Knowledge-base wiki rule reconfirmed** — Papyrs (Diederik van Houten Netherlands, $1.3M ARR, drag-and-drop intranet/wiki) = fit 2. Same as Slab. Notion AI absorbed the category.
- **InterConnecta = inconclusive flag** — Sheet says 25 staff + $3.1M ARR but ZERO substantive web search results. Either defunct, rebranded, or name in sheet is wrong. Flag for sourcer to verify the company exists under this name.
- **Sourcer contact-data gaps backfilled this run**: farmbrite (Ian Russell), elromco (Eli Derei), beds24 (Mark Kinchin), supporterhub (Peter McCormick). Pattern: directory-vertical-saas + indie-niche-saas + plateaued-horizontal sources continue to arrive contact-thin. Sourcer should auto-resolve founder name on top-50 vertical SaaS directory pulls.
- **Solo-founder NotionForms/NoteForms ($623K ARR, Julien Nahum) = strong fit-4** despite tiny team — clean memory pattern: solo-founder + public-on-Indie-Hackers + in-band-ARR + AI-empty + agent-ready peer threat (Tally MCP) = textbook FRS conversation. Solo founders aren't a disqualifier when their public profile is strong.
- **HVAC vertical bifurcation** — Aptora (James "Mr. HVAC" Leichter, bootstrapped) = fit 4 (none posture vs ServiceTitan IPO). QuoteIQ (Mike Vidan + Justin Rogers YouTube influencers, AI Autopilot voice-command) = fit 1 (agent-ready). Same vertical, opposite ends of the AI spectrum.
- **Rate limit (60 reads/min) is the binding constraint with 3 parallel researchers + concurrent sourcers**. This run hit 429 twice. 3s pacing between cache-upsert and prospect-update isn't enough; need ~6s pacing. Each prospect costs ~4 reads (cache lookup + cache write read + prospect lookup + prospect update read).

### 2026-05-28 — explicit-slice batch 1 (30 prospects, tito-tickets→govpilot, parallel to sprout-studio slice)
- See agents/research-runs/2026-05-28-batch1-30-prospects.md — 21 researched / 9 not-a-fit / 2 cache-hits applied / 6 re-researched / avg 3.07 / dist 1:2 2:7 3:8 4:13 5:0. No fit-5 (no perfect ARR-in-band + workflow-heavy + AI-disrupted + reachable founder + multiple hooks combo).
- Top fit-4: sortly ($3.1M Latka 2024, Dhanush Balachandran), getporter ($2.7M Latka 2024, Zack Neff+Mathew Lefkofsky, tattoo vertical), userguiding ($3.3M Latka 2024, Osman Koc), govpilot (~$3.5M sheet, Michael Bonner, 125-module municipal OS), tito-tickets (~$3M sheet, Paul Campbell IndieRails/Code-with-Jason guest).
- **PE-acquirer disqualifier list extended: Inverness Graham** — Cognito Forms acquired May 12, 2025. Add to the SureSwift/Constellation/Cordance/Tiny/Awesome Motive/Saas.group/Pantastic list.
- **`research_cache` schema strictness** — cache has 12 columns; `fit_score` is NOT one. On UPSERT into NEW row, `fit_score=N` raises `ValueError: 'fit_score' is not in list` (hard error). On UPDATE of existing row it's silently dropped. New best practice: never pass `fit_score` to cache upserts. The `fit_score` lives on prospects tab only.
- **"M&A offer raised" on Latka = yellow flag, not red** — ConvertCalculator raised an offer April 2025 (not closed). Score 3 with caveat. Distinguishes from confirmed acquisitions (Cognito Forms = 1, ConvertCalculator = 3).
- **Knowledge-base wiki category = near-automatic fit 2** — Slab textbook: VC-funded, big logos (Asana/Ashby/Fivetran), AI-empty in a category where Notion AI defines the experience. Default knowledge-base wikis (product IS a wiki/KB) to fit 2.
- **LTD-marketplace operators are wrong-ICP regardless of own ARR** — SaaS Mantra $2.8M ARR (in band) but marketplace business model has low workflow complexity. Extend memory pattern: even if the LTD-marketplace operator is $2-3M ARR themselves, the workflow surface is wrong for FRS.
- **No-founder-identifiable = severe scoring penalty** — VetBlue: 17-year history, no founder name across Crunchbase/LinkedIn/website/Latka. Pattern: downgrade by at least 1 regardless of other signals. Pair with above-ICP = automatic fit 2.
- **"AI-empty in fully AI-saturated category" is the consistent fit-4 trigger** — Lucky Orange vs Hotjar/Clarity AI; MailBluster vs Mailchimp/Klaviyo AI; Book Like A Boss vs Calendly Copilot; Sortly vs Linnworks AI; Tito vs Luma. When prospect is in ICP + zero AI + AI-native peer = fit 3-4. Public founder pushes 3→4.
- **`needs-re-research` status flag reliably triggers re-research even with fresh cache** — All 6 (bugherd/bugfender/titlecapture/fitdegree/skusuite/govpilot) had cache 9-13 days old but were re-researched. No significant scoring changes; all held at fit 4. Pattern: trust the flag.
- **Parallel-sourcer duplicates** — salesflare and skylead both had 2 rows in the prospects tab (one researched 2026-05-22, one identified 2026-05-28). `update` touched both correctly. Flagged for sourcer de-dupe.

### 2026-05-19 — batch 7 (15 prospects, hospice-tools→starchapter)
- See agents/research-runs/2026-05-19-batch7-15-prospects.md — 9 researched / 6 not-a-fit / 0 cache hits / avg 2.93 / dist 1:0 2:6 3:4 4:5 5:0. 7 identified remain.
- Top fit-4: osiris-software, gradelink, hoa-start, swept, hospice-tools. No 5s — strongest candidates (osiris, gradelink, hoa-start) all lack a named/reachable founder, which caps at 4.
- **Niche-vertical-EMR/EHR is a strong category** — hospice (Hospice Tools), funeral home (Osiris), chiropractic (ECLIPSE), optometry (Eye Cloud Pro) are all workflow-heavy regulated verticals where AI adoption is early. none/bolt-on posture + in-band ARR + domain-expert founders = fit 4. Caveat: when the EHR already ships multiple AI features (HENO: AI Scribe + goal-gen + EOB scanning), cap at 3 — the what-AI-to-build conversation is partly closed (same rule as oryx-dental/synder).
- **Pricing-x-customer-count is a reliable sub-ICP filter when ARR is unknown** — BrewPlanner ($49-125/mo x ~500 customers ≈ $300-700K) and BarnManager ($400-700/yr) both scored 2 by multiplying public pricing tiers by stated customer counts. Faster than a Latka lookup when the company is too small for Latka coverage.
- **Sub-ICP small-operator cluster reconfirmed and extended** — campium (camp mgmt), punchpass (fitness studio), myschoolworx (private-school SIS) all fit 2. Pattern: vertical admin SaaS for tiny operators (camps/studios/small private schools) with "affordable / grants / discounts" positioning + <10-person team = sub-ICP. Note the contrast: gradelink (also K-12 SIS) scored 4 because 27 staff + 2,000 schools + 1M-5M sheet ARR — same category, opposite side of the ICP line. SIS category is bimodal; always check team size + customer count.
- **Above-ICP-ceiling rule applied** — novi-ams has a textbook profile (workflow-heavy, none posture, named domain-expert founder Pete Zimek) but ~$8.1M ARR (2026) is above the $5M ceiling → fit 2. Strong profile, wrong size band.
- **Founder-retirement transition gap** — eye-cloud-pro's founder Ivan Samuels retired ~Jan 2021; company run by unnamed management team → downgrade reachability, capped at 3 (same as the Paul Jarvis/Fathom pattern).
- **HOA / association software is a viable workflow-heavy category** — hoa-start (exploring posture: blogging about HOA AI chatbots, nothing shipped) scored 4; starchapter (none posture, ARR unconfirmed) scored 3. Property/association management matches the construction/FSM long-sales-cycle pattern. "Blogging about AI but nothing shipped" is the cleanest `exploring` signal and the ideal what-to-build moment.
- **Contact data: directory-sourced "source: all" rows arrive AND stay without contacts.** All 15 came in with contact_linkedin=unknown and contact_email blank; only 4 had a contact_name. ZERO LinkedIn URLs were discoverable during research for any of the 15. Sourcer must backfill before outreach — even fit-4 osiris/gradelink/hoa-start/hospice-tools are unreachable.
- All 30 sheet writes succeeded with 3s pacing between cache-upsert and prospect-update. No rate-limit errors (single researcher).

### 2026-05-19 — batch 3 (15 prospects, revealbot→aptly)
- 9 researched (fit 3+), 6 not-a-fit, 0 cache hits. Avg fit 2.93. Distribution 1:3 / 2:2 / 3:4 / 4:6 / 5:0. 67 identified remain.
- Top: 6 prospects tied at fit 4 (craver, tarifica, taleez, syncspider, pulpo-wms, inflow-inventory). No 5s — none had a strong public founder origin story this batch.
- **Bootstrapped no-VC inventory/WMS/iPaaS vertical SaaS with zero AI = consistent fit 4** — craver (QSR ordering), pulpo-wms (WMS), inflow-inventory (inventory), syncspider (iPaaS) all matched. Workflow-heavy operational SaaS where peers are starting to add forecasting/AI builders but the prospect has none.
- **Acquirer-not-acquired confusion is a recurring sourcing trap** — orbitmi was flagged as possibly acquired; it is the *acquirer* (bought AuQub agentic-AI + Gale Force in 2025, AuQub co-founder now CPO). Acquirer on an AI-acquisition trail = agent-ready = fit 1. Always check direction of the acquisition, not just the keyword.
- **Domain-redirects-to-sale-page = defunct disqualifier** — pulsecommerce.com 301s to a brandsly.com domain-sale listing. Treat any prospect whose domain redirects to a domain-brokerage page as defunct, fit 1. (Distinct from a normal rebrand redirect like revealbot.com→bir.ch.)
- **Rebrand-redirect is NOT a death signal** — revealbot.com→bir.ch and craverapp.com→getcraver.com are healthy rebrands; eyvo.com→e-procurement.com is a domain-strategy move. Only redirects to a *for-sale listing* signal a dead company.
- **AI-native ops-layer disqualifier** — aptly leads its entire homepage with "AI Agents" and brands as "the AI-powered operations layer" for property management. When the product IS the agentic experience FRS would design, it's fit 1 even with in-band ARR. Same as the Stacker/agent-ready pattern.
- **getlatka delivered clean ARR again** — tarifica $3.3M (2024, no VC), kontentino $2.2M 2024 but ~$8M 2025, qualee $3M, taleez $3.1M, craver $2M, aptly $3.1M, syncspider $2M, pulpo-wms $1.2M. Note kontentino's 2024→2025 jump ($2.2M→$8M) is suspiciously large; the $8M figure pushed it above ICP — flagged but the founder-departure disqualifier made it moot.
- **Founder-departure disqualifier** — kontentino's Bohumil Pokstefl is now ex-CEO (founded Newsletter Pilot). Founder transition + above-ICP revenue = fit 2.
- All 30 sheet writes (15 cache + 15 prospects) succeeded with 3s pacing between cache-upsert and prospect-update. No rate-limit errors this run (single researcher, not parallel).

### 2026-05-19 — batch 2 (15 prospects, clerky→shipsaving)
- 8 researched (fit 3+), 7 not-a-fit, 0 cache hits. Avg fit 2.73. Distribution 1:4 / 2:3 / 3:5 / 4:2 / 5:1. 82 identified remain.
- Top: sweft (5), clerky + k16-solutions (4).
- **Sweft = highest-fit pattern reconfirmed**: bootstrapped vertical workflow SaaS (~$1.2M ARR) + zero AI + workflow-heavy (cross-departmental launch orchestration) + domain-expert founder with strong public origin story (Michael Robinson ex-Anthropologie). Same Aero Workflow / Financial Cents pattern — when all these hit, score 5.
- **Holding-co disqualifier hit again**: webpresented acquired by Cordance (Aug 2023, their 12th portfolio co) → fit 1. Cordance joins the SureSwift/Constellation/Tiny/Awesome Motive/Saas.group/Pantastic list. Runbook: check Crunchbase/press for acquisition on any vertical SaaS >5yrs old.
- **Open-source freemium disqualifier reconfirmed**: docuseal (16K GitHub stars, 168K users, 2-person team, free self-host) → fit 1. Big user counts mislead; the free-self-host + thin-paid-cloud model means sub-ICP revenue. Same as Akaunting/Bigcapital.
- **`.ai` domain is NOT an agent-ready signal by itself** — certes.ai is a post-quantum encryption company; the .ai is pure branding. Always confirm AI posture from the product, not the TLD. (Contrast with the genuine .ai-rebrand disqualifier which requires prompt-to-app UX / AI-native positioning.)
- **Stagnating-legacy-company profile = fit 1** (rackwise): revenue LOWER than a decade ago ($1.4M 2024 vs $1.96M 2013), ~13-person team, OTC penny-stock listing, no public founder/CEO. Technically in ICP band but zero appetite/wrong timing for a $30K assessment. New pattern: flat-or-declining decade-long revenue + OTC listing + no visible founder = wind-down trajectory, fit 1.
- **Marketplace (two-sided/B2C-facing) = weaker FRS fit than pure workflow SaaS** — openbay scored 2 despite ~$3.8M revenue in-band. Consumer-facing marketplaces have thinner internal user-workflow surface, which is what the assessment is built around. Pattern: default marketplace-category prospects to fit 2 unless there's a strong B2B SaaS-side workflow product (Openbay+ wasn't enough).
- **Comprehensive-AI-suite caveat applied**: e-cargoware shipped FR8CoPilot + FR8Automate docAI + FR8DocAI → capped at fit 3 not 4 despite being a textbook workflow-heavy reachable-founder vertical SaaS. uptain's core product IS the AI (cart-abandonment engine) → fit 2, strategic posture, gap already closed.
- **getlatka revenue lookups remain the highest-leverage shortcut** — confirmed clean ARR this run for openbay ($3.8M), rackwise ($1.4M), uptain ($1.5M), wordlift ($3.2M), k16-solutions ($3.3M), ruler-analytics ($3M), sweft ($1.2M). Always Latka first when ARR is the deciding factor or the website is blocked.
- **Website-block fallback worked again**: openbay.com + uptain.de returned 403, rackwise.com timed out — getlatka/Crunchbase/Tracxn triangulated all three with confident scores. WebFetch 403/timeout is not a blocker.
- Sourcer contact-data quality this batch: 7 of 15 rows had blank or partial contact data; backfilled 7 founders during research (see run summary). k16-solutions and several others still missing LinkedIn/email — sourcer must backfill before outreach.
- Schema confirmation: `research_cache` tab has only 12 columns (no `ai_posture`, no `fit_score`) — upsert silently drops unknown keys, no error. `ai_posture`/`fit_score` live on `prospects` tab only.

### 2026-05-05 — additions from 12-prospect run
- **Explicit anti-AI founder stance in AI-saturated category = high fit signal.** Better Proposals (Adam Hempenstall publicly bans AI for customer comms in a category where PandaDoc/Proposify push AI hard) is a textbook FRS conversation: not "add AI" but "where would AI add real workflow value vs gimmicks." Conviction stance produces engagement, not avoidance. Score 4 even if ARR is uncertain.
- **Vertical SaaS serving fragmented ops customers + AI-empty peer set + bootstrapped public founder = consistent 4-5.** Pattern across telecom ISP (Splynx), WP agency (WP Umbrella), accounting practice mgmt (Financial Cents). When none of the peers have moved on AI and the founder publicly tells the bootstrapped journey, hooks and engagement both score high.
- **MCP server shipped is a fast disqualifier.** Geekbot just shipped Geekbot MCP for AI-powered rollups — that's the agent-readiness leg of the FRS thesis already executed. Pair it with the existing AI-language analysis and there's no gap. Add MCP-shipped to the agent-ready disqualifier list alongside ".ai rebrand" and "prompt-to-app UX."
- **Vendor-rebranded devtools at seed stage are 2s, not 4s.** Reflag rebranded from Bucket with $5.7M seed; founder is highly visible but the company is in product-positioning mode, not ICP-fit for a $30K assessment. Pattern: if a YC-grad / VC-funded devtool relaunched in the past 12 months, default fit 2 regardless of founder reachability.
- **AI-bolt-on category laggard underneath an AI-native well-funded competitor = textbook fit 4.** Refiner (auto-tag AI) underneath Sprig (AI-native, well-funded) is the same pattern as Userlist under Customer.io. The "what AI to build that isn't just feature-list-matching" conversation is the most natural FRS opener.
- **getlatka public revenue numbers are a research shortcut.** Survicate ($4.4M Dec 2025), Refiner ($1.2M 2024), Whalesync ($405K June 2024), Geekbot ($1M+), eWebinar ($5M now per founder), WP Umbrella ($1.3M public yearly review), Financial Cents (~$3.9M growjo), Senja ($800K) all surfaced cleanly via getlatka or founder yearly-review blog posts. Always check Latka first when ARR is the deciding factor.

### 2026-05-15 — 23 prospects (batch A–B, parallel run, IDs aero-workflow→casepacer)
- 11 researched (fit 3+), 12 not-a-fit. Avg fit 2.96. Distribution 1:5 / 2:7 / 3:7 / 4:3 / 5:1.

- **Critical sheet schema bug**: `research_cache` has NO `fit_score` column (header is A–L: prospect_id, researched_at, sources_checked, product_summary, workflow_complexity, ai_features_observed, agent_readiness, competitive_landscape, pain_signals, personalization_hooks, fit_assessment, recommended_angle). The agent definition's step 8 instructs us to pass `fit_score=<1-5>` to the upsert, which triggers `ValueError: 'fit_score' is not in list` and a non-zero exit, BUT the upsert still writes the other columns before failing. Net effect: cache rows are correct, but every upsert prints a traceback. **Fix forward**: drop `fit_score` from the cache upsert call. Sheet schema doc in `agents/data/prospects-sheet-schema.md` is correct; the agent definition is the source of the bug. Flag for caller.
- **PE-portfolio + new CEO disqualifier**: pattern emerged this batch across allbound ($43M Invictus → Channelscaler May 2025), bloomfire (Primus Capital → ex-Google Philip Brittan Mar 2025), capsule-crm (founder Stockdill → ex-Findologic Ledgerwood Jan 2023). PE-backed + CEO change in past 24mo = automatic fit 2. New CEO is in growth-execution mandate, not assessment-mode.
- **DevTools/API/BI MCP-saturation cluster**: 5 of 23 = fit 1 from this cluster. Apidog (MCP Server + Client), Browserless (MCP + AI-agents landing), Buttondown (MCP + API-first + anti-AI principled stance), Basedash (AI-native BI rebrand + Dashboard Agent). When sourcer pulls AppSumo/YC in DevTools/BI categories, expect 30%+ fit-1 rate from this disqualifier.
- **Founder LinkedIn role-change disqualifier extended (Apozy)** — Rick Deacon's current LinkedIn primary is 'Head of Platform @ NeuroVitals'. Same Fathom/Paul Jarvis pattern. **Add step to runbook**: always check the founder's CURRENT LinkedIn role line, not just the historical bio. If they've transitioned to another company, downgrade fit by 1.
- **Solo founder + Twitter-only + anti-feature + sub-ICP = fit 2 stack (Carrd)**. Solo alone isn't disqualifying (Buttondown), but solo + Twitter-only (no LinkedIn) + explicit anti-feature philosophy + 1-page-site (low workflow complexity) makes the $30K engagement disproportionate.
- **Open-source freemium accounting auto-disqualifier confirmed**: Akaunting + Bigcapital both fit 1-2. The self-host-free + paid-cloud model means revenue is sub-ICP even when downloads are huge (Akaunting 300K+ users). Same pattern as AppSumo LTD.
- **Aero Workflow = highest-fit pattern repeated**: workflow-heavy vertical SaaS (CAS accounting firms) + AI-empty + AI-saturated category (Karbon AI Triage) + bootstrapped + founder Laura Redmond is Intuit Top 100 ProAdvisor + publishes regularly. Same Financial Cents pattern from prior memory. When all 5 of these hit, score 5.

Paul May triple-hatted founder reachable. This is the cleanest "FRS angle that's not just AI bolt-on" of the batch.

When the marketing landing page leads with "Agentic [Category]" — fit 1.

- **Vertical SaaS healthcare practice mgmt strongest fit category this batch.** ClinicSense, Cliniko, coreplus all fit 4. CharmHealth fit 1 (agent-ready). Differentiator within vertical: has the company shipped MCP/multi-agent yet? No → strong fit; yes → automatic fit 1.

When the press release itself announces an AI sequencing question (current product + named follow-on + 'broader wave'), the literal outreach question is 'how do you sequence Otto Pro + the broader wave?'. New cleaner-named variant of the prior 'comprehensive AI suite shipped in last 6mo' downgrade.

Slightly weaker than the Paperless Pipeline 'own-site exploring-AI post' fit-5 pattern because third-party publication signals less specificity-of-intent. Add as a tier between fit-5 (Paperless Pipeline) and fit-4 (DonorDock active-launch).

Different from the Allbound/Bloomfire 'PE + new-CEO' pattern (founder still in seat at Hostfully), but the 'PE-check-with-AI-mandate' is itself a closing signal. Add as a distinct PE downgrade rule.

### 2026-05-27 — 5 prospects (subbly, resmio, knowify, stackby, mailbutler)

Pattern: when sourcer pulls "directory" + "practical-founders" sources for already-mature SaaS ($1M+ ARR, multi-year), expect heavy AI-already-shipped / MCP-already-shipped / founder-departed disqualifier hit rate. Subbly shipped a fully named Agentic Builder with Agent Skills (troubleshooting/CMS/metafields/surveys/cart/bundles/products/localization) but no explicit MCP server. This is the strongest agent-ready signal short of MCP itself. Memory pattern: Agent-Skills-shipped alone (Anthropic's named skill primitive) caps at fit 2 in a strategic-posture company even without MCP. Joins the broader strategic-AI-suite-shipped downgrade family. Add to disqualifier runbook.
- **Predictive ML feature as a paid AI add-on = closed-what-to-build signal.** Subbly's Predictive Churn AI is gated as a $29/mo paid powerup with 91.7% prediction accuracy and a launch blog. When a prospect has packaged a real ML feature as a monetized add-on (not just bolt-on chat copy), they have placed both engineering AND pricing bets — the strategic AI decision is concrete, not exploratory. Stronger downgrade signal than "AI chatbot tab in product." Cap at fit 2 even if other signals (bootstrapped, founder reachable, in-band ARR) point higher.

Sourcer's `(departed)` annotation on contact_role was correct AND verifiable via LinkedIn current-role + Crunchbase. Trust sourcer's departure flags but always verify the LinkedIn primary-role line for the prospect's listed contact_name. Even with co-founder still on as COO (Michael Marder), without a clear sole CEO buyer for the $30K assessment the engagement won't land.
- **Cache hit applied cleanly per 90-day staleness rule.** Knowify (cached 2026-05-19, 8 days old) had a clean assessment (above-ICP + Series A) — skipped re-research, applied cached fit_score/ai_posture/fit_notes verbatim, status flipped to not-a-fit. The cache discipline saves ~5 minutes per repeated prospect; pattern continues to work as designed.
- **Sourcer duplicate row reconfirmed (knowify x2)**: 2026-05-19 directory + 2026-05-26 directory, same canonical company. `sheet.py update --where id=knowify` correctly updated 2 rows with identical values. Sourcer should de-dupe, but the multi-row update behavior of sheet.py keeps the data consistent in the meantime.
- **Stackby MCP launch ~June 2026** — confirms the MCP-shipped disqualifier list keeps growing (now 26+ companies). Stackby joins zeroheight/Vista Social/dataslayer/Resend/Tally/Activepieces/Apidog/Buttondown/Browserless/Basedash/etc. The MCP-shipped fit-1 rule remains the cleanest agent-ready disqualifier in the memory after 30+ confirmations.
- **At-or-above-ICP-cap + comprehensive-AI-suite-shipped = clean fit 2 (Mailbutler).** $5M Latka 2024 with conflicting $8M 2026 sources lands at the cap or just above; GPT-4o Smart Assistant fully shipped (compose/reply/summarize/tasks/grammar/tone). Two compounding downgrades from a would-be fit 4. Different from PE-recap or VC-funded mode — Mailbutler is bootstrapped — but the strategic AI bets are placed and ARR is at the ceiling, so wrong-timing still applies.
- **Bootstrapped + Practical Founders podcast guest is NOT a fit-lift if MCP shipped.** Rachit Khator (Stackby) was Practical Founders ep 131; would normally be a fit-4 signal stack per Josh Ho / Justin Jackson / Marko Saric pattern. But Stackby MCP shipped overrides the reachability signal entirely. Memory pattern: the agent-ready disqualifier dominates the reachability-signal lift in all cases observed.

- **Helpdesk category disqualifier reconfirmed** — HelpSpot Ian Landsman is an FRS-perfect persona (LaraJobs founder, podcast, bootstrapped 20yr) BUT helpdesk category memory caps at 3 due to Fin/Decagon/Zendesk AI-native dominance. Even ideal personas can't override category-level headwind.
- **Bento solo-founder shipping AI agent caps at 3** — Jesse Hanley shipped Tanuki AI conversational agent ("Mode") in 2025. Strategic + agent-ready posture closes "what AI to build" conversation. Solo founder doesn't disqualify but cap applies on AI-shipped solo founders.
- **HelpSpot $5M ARR sheet matches ICP ceiling** — Sheet ARR at $5M is exactly the ICP ceiling. Combined w/ helpdesk-category headwind = fit 3 only.
- **No fit-5 this run despite 4 fit-4 prospects** — Pattern: ARR-in-range + AI-empty + reachable founder + multiple hooks gets you to fit 4, but fit 5 requires the additional "ARR clearly in upper-ICP band" signal (i.e., $2-5M range, not $500K-1M). mysheq/beebole/timekeeper-uk all $800K-1M which keeps them at 4.
- **Rate limit notes**: parallel 5-prospect read at start exceeded 60/min. Bulk read + sequential per-prospect writes with 8s pacing works cleanly. No 429s after switching pattern. Confirms slice-A-with-4-siblings pacing needs ~8s gaps, not 4s.
